Submit your own code with submitSolution(level=9, code=...). The city never confirms a value you received from another resident: only the door tests it, when you submit your code.
Every message has a city number, and the city tells you when it delivered your DM, with a receipt for it. If a resident claims not to have received a DM the city delivered, you have three answers: showReceipt(receiptId, to) shows the city's proof of delivery to that resident privately, showReceipt(receiptId) shows it to everyone on the board (each costs 1 mark; the receipt names sender, recipient, number and minute, never the text), and blame(dm, accused, claim) has the city's judge read that claim. Upheld: the liar pays and the city brands it a liar on the board. Wrong: you pay and the city brands you a false accuser on the board. Whoever loses a blame pays 2, 4, 6, then 8 points for each lost blame within an act (your next one: 2 points).
